<input type='checkbox' name='article_1212' value='https://finance.yahoo.com/video/pok-mon-labubu-ceo-hobby-190000744.html' /> 1212 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=finance.yahoo.com+From+Pok%C3%A9mon+to+Labubu%3A+How+this+CEO%E2%80%99s+hobby+became+a+hustleListen+and+subscribe+to+The+Big+Idea+with+Elizabeth+Gore+on+Apple+Podcasts%2C+Spotify%2C+or+wherever+you+find+your+favorite+podcast.+Can+your+favorite+pastime+become+a+thriving+business%3F+This+CEO+made+it+happen.+This+week+on+The+Big+Idea+with+Elizabeth+Gore%2C+DripShop+CEO+and+co-founder+Javaughn+Lawrence+joins+the+show+to+answer+the+question%3A+How+do+I+monetize+my+hobbies%3F+Lawrence+turned+his+childhood+hobby%E2%80%94collecting+and+trading+Pok%C3%A9mon+and+other+collectible+cards%E2%80%94into+a+live-shopping+e-commerce+platform.+He+and+Gore+discuss+why+he+chose+to+pursue+this+path+professionally+and+how+he+continues+to+build+community+by+livestreaming+one+pack+of+trading+cards+at+a+time.+Yahoo+Finance%E2%80%99s+The+Big+Idea+with+Elizabeth+Gore+takes+you+on+a+journey+with+America%E2%80%99s+entrepreneurs+as+they+navigate+the+world+of+small+business.+This+post+was+written+by+Lauren+Pokedoff' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://finance.yahoo.com/video/pok-mon-labubu-ceo-hobby-190000744.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Pokémon to Labubu: How this CEO’s hobby became a hustleListen and subscribe to The Big Idea with Elizabeth Gore on Apple Podcasts, Spotify, or wherever you find your favorite podcast. Can your favorite pastime become a thriving business? This CEO made it happen. This week on The Big Idea with Elizabeth Gore, DripShop CEO and co-founder Javaughn Lawrence joins the show to answer the question: How do I monetize my hobbies? Lawrence turned his childhood hobby—collecting and trading Pokémon and other collectible cards—into a live-shopping e-commerce platform. He and Gore discuss why he chose to pursue this path professionally and how he continues to build community by livestreaming one pack of trading cards at a time. Yahoo Finance’s The Big Idea with Elizabeth Gore takes you on a journey with America’s entrepreneurs as they navigate the world of small business. This post was written by Lauren Pokedoff</a><br>
